- **common-changelog**: Before you create or change `CHANGELOG.md`, use this skill. When a prompt says "changelog", "changelog entry", "review the changelog", "check the changelog", or "release notes", use this skill. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Communication-Research-Skills/skills/commres-theory-building) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **commres-topic-selection**: Use when deciding whether a communication project fits Communication Research (CR) and is shaped as quantitative, theory-testing social science. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Communication-Research-Skills/skills/commres-topic-selection) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)

Prepares the materials; it does not waive requirements. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Communication-Research-Skills/skills/commres-transparency-and-data) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
